technologies = {

	sov_fighter_1 = {

		allow_branch = {
			NOT = { has_dlc = "By Blood Alone" }
		}

		enable_equipments = {
			sov_fighter_equipment_1
		}

		path = {
			leads_to_tech = sov_fighter_2
			research_cost_coeff = 1
		}

		path = {
			leads_to_tech = sov_fighter_multirole_1
			research_cost_coeff = 1
		}

		research_cost = 2.5
		start_year = 1934

		folder = {
			name = sov_air_techs_folder
			position = { x = 4 y = 2 }
		}

		categories = {
			major_technologies
			light_air
			cat_fighter
			air_equipment
			cat_yak_planes
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 1

			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						WA_AI_RESEARCH_needs_fighters = yes
						WA_AI_RESEARCH_needs_jet_fighters = yes
						WA_AI_RESEARCH_needs_multirole_fighters = yes
					}
				}
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = { WA_rb_uses_major_technologies > 0 }
							check_variable = { WA_rb_uses_light_air > 0 }
							check_variable = { WA_rb_uses_cat_fighter > 0 }
							check_variable = { WA_rb_uses_air_equipment > 0 }
							check_variable = { WA_rb_uses_cat_yak_planes > 0 }
							check_variable = { WA_rb_uses_t_sov_fighter_1 > 0 }
						}
					}
					date < 1933.1.1
				}
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = { WA_rb_ahead2_major_technologies > 0 }
							check_variable = { WA_rb_ahead2_light_air > 0 }
							check_variable = { WA_rb_ahead2_cat_fighter > 0 }
							check_variable = { WA_rb_ahead2_air_equipment > 0 }
							check_variable = { WA_rb_ahead2_cat_yak_planes > 0 }
							check_variable = { WA_rb_ahead2_t_sov_fighter_1 > 0 }
						}
					}
					date < 1932.1.1
				}
				OR = {
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = WA_AI_unused_research_slots }
						date < 1934.1.1
					}
					AND = {
						has_country_flag = WA_AI_unused_research_slots
						date < 1933.1.1
					}
				}
			}
		}

			# WA_RB_CONSUME begin - generated by tools/gen/gen_research_bonus_tracking.py, do not edit
